# Bannerlord Reign Roadmap

Master project backlog for Bannerlord Reign. This file is the source of truth for planned, active, and completed work.

## How To Use

- `[ ]` Planned or incomplete.
- `[x]` Implemented and verified.
- Add new requests to **Inbox** until they are reviewed and placed.
- Keep unfinished follow-up work separate even when its parent feature is complete.

## Inbox

- [ ] Add a negotiated NPC action to leave their spouse by letter.
  - 2026-10-23 request: Use the existing action/agreement system, like duels and trades. Acceptance means writing to the current spouse that they are no more and ending the marriage; it promises nothing about going with, joining, marrying or romancing the player. Give hidden action-request guidance that this is a major, consequential decision. The person leaving suffers a huge relation loss with the spouse and the spouse's clan.
  - 2026-10-23 implementation and offline verification: Exact spouse/clan-bound `leave_spouse` request, ordinary mutual signatures/refusal/counteroffer, fixed -50 native relation with the former spouse and each other living adult member of the spouse's clan (excluding the leaver, spouse counted once), reciprocal authoritative affinity consequences, native spouse/romance cleanup, durable private notice and idempotent receipts. No movement or clan transfer. Changed/Release Tier 3 `20261023-232342-1dae1dda` passed all 11 operations, 2,425 MCP tests and enforced paired hygiene in 655.726 seconds. Exact-artifact offline contracts `verify-1792798500177-7e6abb99` passed 114/114, including 224/224 structured-action assertions. Evidence: `.codex-build/spouse-letter-01a16a97/RESULTS.md`. Native consent/effects/save-reload acceptance remains pending, so the item stays unchecked; nothing deployed.

- [ ] Qualify NPC-memory embedding alternatives against representative Reign history before considering a model switch.

MiniLM reduced isolated worker peak RSS469.5->362.6MiB and first-question semantic-search p9536.4->18.8ms, but full retrieval/writer-request p95 was2041.6->2212.4ms (no speedup observed). Corrected isolated generation metadata and required owned-document eligibility probes; Tier4 initial validation and Tier2 repair/artifact replay passed. Evidence: `.codex-build/npc-memory-pipeline-01a1459f/RESULTS.md`. Retain BGE and keep qualification open for held-out relevance/abstention, representative saved derivatives and native/concurrent population, target-hardware performance and migration/rollback acceptance. No runtime switch or deployment.
  - 2026-10-18 full-pipeline request: Compare MiniLM against BGE through actual vector worker/index/filter/cache/HTTP, SQL fusion and final writer packing, preserving complete recall and rejecting unrelated memories while measuring latency/RAM. Isolated source-linked derivative fixtures and nonce worker route are in development; reuse unchanged histories, separate first/repeated queries, retain failures and keep installed model/settings/campaigns unchanged. Qualification remains open pending measured results and representative acceptance.
  - 2026-10-16 replay follow-up: Product validation passed, then the explicit prompt-efficiency suite exposed the same isolated native-profile gap on a generated continuation repair request. Extended fixture-only binding to captured repairs and added named/pronoun repair contracts; fresh validation is required. Model-ranking measurements are unchanged; failed evidence is retained in the comparison folder.
  - 2026-10-16 current-system comparison: The user requested actual comparison results. Ran the installed Linux worker dependencies against397 frozen real original turns from15 conversation histories,100 exact lookup controls and33 source-checked natural questions. Native-window dense Hit@5 is78.8% BGE,87.9% MiniLM,81.8% Granite and84.8% Bekko; Linux p95 is34.5/11.6/13.8/10.3ms respectively. None supplies all required original sources for any of seven difficult callbacks at a five-result limit. Existing current-artifact SQL replay passed107 cases plus33 natural queries; final writer-envelope capture exposed an isolated native political-profile binding gap, whose repair preserves the required guard and fixture identity. Full receipts, final comparison and coverage limits belong in `.codex-build/npc-memory-current-01a1459f/RESULTS.md`. Keep qualification open: reused English development probes are not independent multilingual, concurrent/native, privacy or migration acceptance; no runtime model switch.
  - 2026-10-16 request: Compare embedding approaches and build a reproducible benchmark covering personal memories, relationships, historical events and irrelevant memories; measure retrieval accuracy, latency and memory use across a large NPC population. Treat SkyrimNet Beta27's reported English top-five results of65%/71%/76% and Russian46%/88%/93% for MiniLM/Granite/Bekko,234MB/442MB Bekko/Granite downloads, relevance changes and background re-embedding/load-failure rollback as external evidence, not Reign measurements.
  - 2026-10-16 implementation: Added an isolated, provider-free benchmark with97 synthetic memories,32 bilingual queries, independent-topic dev/test splits,72 privacy/time/timeline/campaign/generation/status traps, graded ranking/abstention metrics, dev-only thresholds, per-stage latency/RSS and pinned BGE/MiniLM/Granite/Bekko-a8m artifacts. Actual Reign baseline is384-dimensional BGE-small FastEmbed; common ONNX comparison separates adapter effects. Synthetic experiments and canonical verification are documented in `ReignServer/docs/server/NpcMemoryRetrievalBenchmark.md`. Keep this qualification item open for representative private data, independent labels, production hybrid/packing replay, realistic concurrent load and safe model-versioned re-embedding/rollback proof; no runtime switch or deployment.

- [ ] Add party-only announcements with remembered individual reactions on later approach.
  - 2026-10-11 clarification: Every announcement heard since the previous conversation must inform the next eligible approach, without the twelve-item recent-memory cap dropping pending subjects. Characters may combine their thoughts, comment on some, or deliberately say nothing and continue their day. Save that independent choice only with an accepted response; interrupted/fallback replies retain pending subjects, and announcements arriving during generation remain for the next review. Implementation and native acceptance of this refinement are in progress.
  - 2026-10-11 request and approval draft: Send one player announcement to the actual main-party roster, including eligible guests, without any immediate NPC replies. Freeze recipients at send time; exclude town/keep residents, other parties, absent members and prisoners. Preserve exact audience-scoped history and prompt each hearer's later character-specific response while respecting one-hour silent chat resumption. No announcement-text action execution or automatic rewards. [Full plan](docs/agent/NPC_LIVES_AND_STORYLINES_PLAN.md). Implementation and privacy/native acceptance remain open.

- [x] Add a kingdom-wide catastrophe and prosperity event system.
  - Once per campaign day, make one saved, idempotent `1%` global event roll. On success, roll `50/50` for harmful versus beneficial, select an eligible event archetype, and only then roll the affected kingdom from the archetype's eligible kingdom pool. A successful global roll should try another archetype of the same polarity if the first has no valid kingdom rather than silently choosing an invalid target or reversing the polarity.
  - Initial harmful catalog: **Famine** reduces food production across the kingdom, **Pestilence** reduces village hearths daily, **Crime Wave** reduces town and castle security daily, **Trade Collapse** reduces prosperity daily, **Construction Stagnation** slows building construction across the kingdom, **Political Unrest** reduces town and castle loyalty daily, and **Border Crisis** immediately begins one valid war.
  - Initial beneficial catalog: **Bountiful Harvest** mirrors Famine, **Population Boom** mirrors Pestilence, **Law and Order** mirrors Crime Wave, **Trade Boom** mirrors Trade Collapse, **Golden Age** mirrors Construction Stagnation by accelerating building construction, **National Unity** mirrors Political Unrest by increasing town and castle loyalty daily, **Grand Reconciliation** immediately ends one valid war, and **Orderly Succession** makes an eligible NPC ruler voluntarily step down in favor of the ruling clan's valid heir.
  - Implemented baseline, subject to future balance tuning: timed economic events last `30` campaign days; only one timed catastrophe or boon may affect a kingdom at once; effects follow the kingdom's current settlements as ownership changes; and expired/destroyed-kingdom effects are cleaned up. Effects are `+/-35%` bound-village food contribution, `+/-1` hearth per village per day with a safe lower floor, `+/-0.5` security per town/castle per day, `+/-1` prosperity per town/castle per day, `+/-25%` building-construction speed, and `+/-0.5` loyalty per town/castle per day.
  - War eligibility must choose an active kingdom and a distinct active non-enemy partner, while preserving any treaties or truces the system is not explicitly authorized to break. Peace eligibility must choose only kingdoms currently at war and then one current enemy; exclude Reign-owned rebellion/civil-war pairs whose resolution is controlled by the rebellion system. Immediate diplomatic events should use the existing authoritative native diplomacy path and record their catastrophe-event origin.
  - Succession eligibility must be computed before the final kingdom roll: NPC-led ruling clan, living ruler, and at least one valid living adult heir. Never forcibly retire or replace the player character. Implement living abdication without Bannerlord's death-only relationship effects, accidental ruler death, or loss of unrelated property, and preserve the ruling clan while transferring leadership to the selected heir.
  - Persist the last rolled day plus active and historical event records so save/load cannot duplicate or reroll a resolved day. Show a kingdom event announcement with exact effect and duration, include active effects in economic explanations, record immediate outcomes, send major events into World History so dialogue can know about them, and provide debug controls that can force each archetype without changing the production `1%` chance.
  - Verification must cover deterministic daily odds and roll ordering, `50/50` polarity, last-step eligibility filtering, no-target fallback, save/load idempotency, timed modifier math and cleanup, ownership changes, kingdom destruction, simultaneous native economic modifiers, treaty-safe war, immediate ordinary peace, civil-war exclusion, NPC succession, player exclusion, announcement/history output, and forced-event debug paths.

  - 2026-07-13: Implemented the workspace-only player-reputation foundation after its design pass. It is independent of native traits and records public player deeds on five signed `-50..+50` axes: Presence, Martial, Statecraft, Judgement, and Decorum. Ordinary/notable/major/defining evidence changes an axis by `1/2/5/10`; tournament wins and losses are `+2/-2` Martial. Positive impressions begin drifting one point toward zero after 15 days without reinforcing positive evidence, while negative impressions recover after 30 days without reinforcing negative evidence. Private events do not enter public reputation until witnessed, exposed, or rumored; rumors are confidence-weighted and can be traced or countered only through the appointed Spymaster using Charm and Roguery. Added timeline-aware SQLite evidence/history, save-backed profile caches, observer-specific learned snapshots behind the identity gate, limited directional relationship effects that never invent debt or dependence, prompt context, APIs, an eighth Court `Reputation` tab, and Spymaster controls. Both projects build with zero warnings; reputation tests pass 11/11 and the full offline pipeline passes 10/10 aggregate checks, including identity 13/13 and rumors 5/5. Keep incomplete until the remaining deed adapters, Spymaster operation presentation, save/reload, and live-campaign effects are verified. The feature remains default-off and was not deployed.

Compound alliance packages now activate the alliance and may include verified transfers, guarantees, military commitments, or a real ruling-clan marriage. Server prompts now use a deterministic maximum of eight valid marriage pairs per relevant counterparty (and only the two negotiating kingdoms for responses), while full authoritative world data remains available for final validation. The provider's exact generic gateway rejection now receives bounded immediate retries before the game is told diplomacy paused. The native snapshot builder is also capped at twelve suitable pairs per kingdom pair, reducing future game-to-server payloads after the next client restart. Both projects build with zero warnings, diplomacy tests pass `51/51`, and the matching server is deployed and healthy; the client DLL remains staged until the currently running Bannerlord process releases it.

Only that selected couple is shown to the LLM; native eligibility is revalidated before execution, and legacy clients are compacted to one pair server-side for compatibility. Diplomacy contracts pass `52/52`, including exact highest-value couple selection and responder pair scoping.

## Reputation And Presentation

- [ ] Build personal reputation separately from native relation.
  - 2026-07-13: Implemented the independent player-only evidence/history, five-axis signed profile, decay/recovery, observer-knowledge, prompt, relationship-modifier, UI, API, and persistence foundation without using or moving native player traits. Keep open for full deed-source coverage and live verification.
  - 2026-07-13: Included this foundation in the authorized Court live-test deployment. It remains player-only, independent of native traits, gated by the default-off Court setting, and incomplete pending live verification and full deed adapter coverage.
  - 2026-07-13: Replaced the former day-threshold reputation decay with symmetric seasonal drift tied to Reign's 126-day calendar. At each new 31.5-day season, every positive axis moves 5 points toward zero and every negative axis moves 5 points toward zero, with clamping so no score crosses neutral. The timeline-aware profile stores the last processed Reign season, makes repeated same-season daily ticks idempotent, catches up missed season boundaries, and establishes a no-decay baseline when an existing save first receives the new field. Seasonal changes increment the profile revision when a score changes so observer knowledge and relationship effects can refresh. Deployed matching client/server hashes; all three projects build with zero warnings; reputation passes 11/11 including exact `+5 -> 0`, `-10 -> -5`, and same-season replay coverage; Court remains 20/20, quick verification 15/15, focused regressions 20/20, and the live campaign schema/profile smoke test passes. Keep incomplete for in-game season-boundary verification and remaining deed-source coverage.
- [ ] Link public reputation to pre-introduction identity recognition, with one observer-specific recognition attempt per encounter until it succeeds.
  - Depends on the directional acquaintance and identity-knowledge network under **Memory, Knowledge, And Rumors**; this item cannot be completed until that network consumes the reputation evaluator.
  - 2026-07-12: Added the reputation-recognition boundary and encounter retry rules. It intentionally returns `unavailable` until the full reputation system supplies public scope, observer awareness, presentation, and disguise evaluation.
  - 2026-07-13: Connected public notoriety to the directional identity gate. Unknown observers receive at most one deterministic recognition attempt per encounter; failure exposes neither canonical identity nor reputation, while success verifies identity through public reputation before storing the observer's learned profile snapshot. Offline identity tests pass 13/13. Keep open for clothing, disguise, status-presentation, and live encounter verification.

## Settlements And Economy

- [ ] Make war consume local manpower and leave persistent food and security damage.
  - Ordinary player and AI volunteers cost `0.5` hearth each without reducing a village below `10`; upgrades, transfers, prisoners, mercenaries, scripted troops, and new-campaign initialization are exempt. Post-start NPC lord parties receive only a culturally appropriate tier-1-to-3 household retinue of `10-15` troops before recruiting normally.
  - A looted village supplies no bound-settlement food and starts at `0%` agricultural recovery when it returns to Normal. Each daily `1d10` roll of `8-10` restores `10%`, scaling its native `6/12/18` food contribution until full recovery; another successful raid resets it to zero.
  - Every recovering village independently applies `-2 x unrecovered fraction` daily security. Siege aftermath additionally applies one-time security losses of `-5` for mercy, `-15` for pillage, and `-30` for devastation. Guard-house patrols draw and replenish real troops from their home garrison instead of spawning replacements.
  - Ordinary recruitment also creates saved village mobilization strain: one point per troop, recovering by `5% + 0.5` daily. At `10%/20%/35%` of current hearth it temporarily removes `1/2/4` additional village food per day, never more than that village's recovered native output. Villages below `30%` agricultural recovery cannot recruit.
  - Each kingdom, or an independent clan across its own fiefs, conservatively redistributes real fortification food stocks. Distressed settlements enter below `30%` or a projected seven-day critical reserve and leave at `50%`; donors enter above `70%`, have nonnegative food change, retain at least `60%`, and ship at most `10` food daily. Recipients receive at most `15` food daily, routes beyond `180` map units and besieged destinations are blocked, and distance/security reduce delivery to `70-90%` while donors pay the full shipment. Received relief gives `+0.05` loyalty; continuing starvation after relief gives `-0.10`, capped with all relief modifiers at `+/-0.20` daily.

Reign derives continuous MBTI axes from the existing 43 trait percentages, uses all 256 ordered JobCannon compatibility scores, subtracts 10 from every positive-roll chance (`36..83`), and rolls each direction independently: `d100 <= chance` immediately changes that observer's affinity by `+1d10`, while a higher roll changes it by `-1d10`. Native NPC relation targets the rounded average of both directional affinities, while player relationships remain native-authoritative and receive only an NPC-perception tag. Threshold pools support asymmetric enemies, rivals, friends, confidants, and attachments; romantic tags and passive romance events require both NPC directions at `70+`. Adult co-present NPCs now keep one directional MBTI affinity pair, roll independent `d100` and signed `1d10` daily changes, project native NPC relation from the directional average, and drift all directional/player-native relationships one point toward zero every five days. Mutual `70+` creates lovers unless related; married-to-another or same-sex pairs use one 30% exception roll. Lovers persist until either direction drops below 30, roll 3% daily co-present conception and one-time 5% rumors, and become an active affair when either has another spouse. Affair discovery queues exact `-50` native-relation changes from the betrayed spouse toward both participants. Mutual `85+` unmarried pairs roll 75% daily marriage; one spouse at `-30` creates estrangement with a one-time 5% rumor, and `-50` queues divorce regardless of the reverse opinion, adding persistent divorced tags and an immediate rumor. Political marriage remains unchanged. Letters and completed interactions produce idempotent small native-relation changes without facets or LLM calls.
